Implement the following plan:

# Fix Subjective Queue Policy: Clear Ownership Boundaries

## Context

Under_target and stale subjective dimensions both correctly defer mid-cycle when objective backlog exists. The bug: **under_target items are never reliably re-injected when objective backlog clears**. They get evicted and stay gone.

Root cause: three sync functions (sync_unscored, sync_stale, auto_cluster_sync) all manipulate the same queue with overlapping authority — sync_unscored prunes things sync_stale manages, and auto_cluster_sync re-evicts what sync_stale just injected.

## Design: Clarify Ownership

Each function owns exactly one domain:

| Function | Owns | Does NOT do |
|----------|------|-------------|
| sync_unscored | Prune/inject unscored IDs | Touch stale or under_target |
| sync_stale | Prune/inject stale + under_target IDs | — |
| auto_cluster_sync | Create clusters, track defer_meta | Evict from queue_order |

The `SubjectiveVisibility` policy snapshot (computed once per operation) ensures all three agree on objective backlog state.

## Changes

### 1. sync_unscored: stop pruning what it doesn't own — `engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py`

**Line 174** (mid-cycle path):
```python
# Before: prune ALL subjective IDs
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)

# After: only prune IDs that are unscored-owned (leave stale/under_target alone)
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
```
Requires adding `under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)` call.

**Line 176** (non-mid-cycle path):
```python
# Before: keep unscored + stale (under_target gets pruned!)
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)

# After: keep everything that's in any active subjective category
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
```

sync_unscored now only removes IDs that have left ALL subjective categories (e.g., dimension scored above target and not stale).

### 2. Remove eviction from auto_cluster_sync — `engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py`

**Lines 274-284**: Remove the eviction block entirely. sync_stale is the single authority on queue membership.

```python
# Remove this block:
else:
    defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
    plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
    objective_evict = [
        fid for fid in order
        if fid in under_target_ids or fid in stale_state_ids
    ]
    for fid in objective_evict:
        order.remove(fid)
        changes += 1

# Keep the defer_state tracking but not the eviction:
else:
    defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
    plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
```

sync_stale already handles eviction via its keep_ids logic (lines 235-241). The auto_cluster_sync eviction was redundant and could undo sync_stale's decisions.

### 3. Pass cycle_just_completed through import path — `app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`

**Line 170-188**: Compute and pass `cycle_just_completed`:

```python
cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")

if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:
    stale_sync_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_stale_dimensions(
        plan, state, policy=policy,
        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
    )
    ...
    auto_cluster_changes = int(plan_queue_mod.auto_cluster_issues(
        plan, state, target_strict=target_strict, policy=policy,
        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
    ))
```

### 4. Separate category tracking in defer_meta — `engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py`

**Lines 204-206**: Track stale and under_target separately:
```python
deferred_stale_ids = sorted(
    fid for fid in stale_candidate_ids
)
deferred_under_target_ids = sorted(
    fid for fid in under_target_queue_ids
)
deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(
    set(deferred_stale_ids) | set(deferred_under_target_ids)
)
```

Add `deferred_stale_ids` and `deferred_under_target_ids` lists to the defer_state dict so messaging can distinguish categories.

### 5. Messaging — minor label changes

- `app/commands/next/render_support.py:25`: Change "Optional re-review" → "Below-target re-review"
- No other messaging changes needed — the queue fix means status/next/plan will naturally agree

### 6. Tests

- Update `tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py`: under_target should NOT be pruned by sync_unscored
- Update `tests/plan/test_auto_cluster_sync_direct.py`: eviction removed from auto_cluster_sync
- **New regression test**: full cycle — evict mid-cycle → clear backlog → verify re-injection
- Existing tests for sync_stale behavior: unchanged (stale+under_target still follow same rules)

## Implementation Order

1. `dimensions.py` — fix sync_unscored ownership (root cause)
2. `auto_cluster_sync.py` — remove eviction, separate defer_meta tracking
3. `plan_sync.py` — pass cycle_just_completed in import path
4. `render_support.py` — label change
5. Tests
6. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q`

## Critical Files

- `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py` — sync_unscored and sync_stale
- `desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py` — cluster sync, eviction removal, defer_meta
- `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py` — import path
- `desloppify/app/commands/next/render_support.py` — cluster label

## Verification

1. `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q` — full suite passes
2. New regression test validates the exact failure scenario
3. `python -m desloppify --lang python scan --path desloppify` then `plan` — verify queue state

    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:

    78→        pruned.append(fid)

    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()

    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)

    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:

   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)

   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→
   135→    1. **Prune** — always remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order``
   136→       that are no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs —
   137→       that is ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).  Pruning runs even
   138→       mid-cycle to clean up items that were incorrectly injected by older
   139→       code that lacked the mid-cycle guard.
   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """
   144→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   151→    )
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→

    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""

    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes

   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   166→            plan,
   167→            clusters,
   168→            existing_by_key,
   169→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   170→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   171→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   172→            description=description,
   173→            action=action,
   174→            now=now,
   175→        )
   176→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   177→
   178→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   179→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   180→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   182→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   183→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   184→            plan,
   185→            clusters,
   186→            existing_by_key,
   187→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   188→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   189→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   190→            description=description,
   191→            action=action,
   192→            now=now,
   193→        )
   194→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   195→
   196→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(
   197→        fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid
   201→        for fid in stale_state_ids
   202→        if fid not in unscored_state_ids and fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   203→    )
   204→    deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(
   205→        set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids)
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   209→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   210→    ut_prune = [
   211→        fid
   212→        for fid in prev_ut_ids
   213→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   214→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   215→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   216→        and fid in order
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in ut_prune:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        changes += 1
   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   225→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   226→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   227→        description = (
   228→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   229→            f"dimensions under target score"
   230→        )
   231→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   232→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   233→            plan,
   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],
   237→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   238→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   239→            description=description,
   240→            action=action,
   241→            now=now,
   242→            optional=True,
   243→        )
   244→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   245→
   246→        existing_order = set(order)
   247→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   248→            if fid not in existing_order:
   249→                order.append(fid)
   250→
   251→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   252→        if deferred_subjective_ids:
   253→            defer_state = update_defer_state(
   254→                plan.get(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   255→                state=state,
   256→                deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids),
   257→                options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   258→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   259→                    now=now,
   260→                ),
   261→            )
   262→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   263→                defer_state,
   264→                state=state,
   265→                options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   266→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   267→                    now=now,
   268→                ),
   269→            )
   270→            if escalated:
   271→                defer_state[_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = list(deferred_subjective_ids)
   272→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   273→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)
   274→            else:
   275→                defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   276→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   277→                objective_evict = [
   278→                    fid
   279→                    for fid in order
   280→                    if fid in under_target_ids or fid in stale_state_ids
   281→                ]
   282→                for fid in objective_evict:
   283→                    order.remove(fid)
   284→                    changes += 1
   285→        else:
   286→            _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   287→    else:
   288→        _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   289→
   290→    return changes
   291→
   292→
   293→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   294→

     2→

    20→def _print_review_import_sync(
    21→    state: dict,

    53→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
    54→    print(colorize("  View broader backlog:  desloppify backlog", "dim"))
    55→    print()

    61→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
    62→    print(colorize(
    63→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
    64→        "dim",
    65→    ))

    67→
    68→def sync_plan_after_import(
    69→    state: dict,
    70→    diff: dict,
    71→    assessment_mode: str,
    72→    *,
    73→    state_file: str | Path | None = None,
    74→    config: dict | None = None,
    75→    import_file: str | None = None,
    76→    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    77→) -> None:
    78→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
    79→    try:
    80→        plan_path = None
    81→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
    82→        if state_file is not None:
    83→            plan_path = plan_queue_mod.plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
    84→        if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan(plan_path):
    85→            return
    86→
    87→        plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
    88→        dirty = False
    89→        workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
    90→        policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(
    91→            state,
    92→            target_strict=target_strict,
    93→            plan=plan,
    94→        )
    95→
    96→        snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    97→        current_scores = plan_queue_mod.ScoreSnapshot(
    98→            strict=snapshot.strict,
    99→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   100→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   101→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   102→        )
   103→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   104→
   105→        communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
   106→            plan,
   107→            state,
   108→            policy=policy,
   109→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   110→            current_scores=current_scores,
   111→        )
   112→        if communicate_result.changes:
   113→            dirty = True
   114→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")
   115→
   116→        has_review_issue_delta = (
   117→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   118→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   119→            or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
   120→        )
   121→        assessment_keys = (
   122→            imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
   123→            if isinstance(import_payload, dict)
   124→            else set()
   125→        )
   126→        import_result = None
   127→        covered_ids = [
   128→            f"subjective::{dim_key}"
   129→            for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
   130→        ]
   131→        stale_sync_result = None
   132→        auto_cluster_changes = 0
   133→
   134→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   135→        if communicate_result.changes:
   136→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   137→
   138→        import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(
   139→            plan,
   140→            state,
   141→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   142→            import_file=import_file,
   143→            import_payload=import_payload,
   144→        )
   145→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   146→            dirty = True
   147→            if import_scores_result.injected:
   148→                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
   149→                injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   150→
   151→        create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(
   152→            plan,
   153→            state,
   154→            policy=policy,
   155→        )
   156→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   157→            dirty = True
   158→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
   159→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   160→
   161→        if has_review_issue_delta:
   162→            import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
   163→                plan,
   164→                state,
   165→                policy=policy,
   166→            )
   167→            if import_result is not None:
   168→                dirty = True
   169→
   170→        if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:
   171→            # Assessment-bearing imports can change subjective queue semantics
   172→            # even when they add no review findings. Keep queue_order aligned
   173→            # first, then rebuild the derived auto-clusters from that queue.
   174→            stale_sync_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_stale_dimensions(
   175→                plan,
   176→                state,
   177→                policy=policy,
   178→            )
   179→            if stale_sync_result.changes:
   180→                dirty = True
   181→
   182→            auto_cluster_changes = int(plan_queue_mod.auto_cluster_issues(
   183→                plan,
   184→                state,
   185→                target_strict=target_strict,
   186→                policy=policy,
   187→            ))
   188→            if auto_cluster_changes:
   189→                dirty = True
   190→
   191→        if dirty:
   192→            if communicate_result.changes:
   193→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   194→                    plan,
   195→                    "sync_communicate_score",
   196→                    actor="system",
   197→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   198→                )
   199→            if import_scores_result.changes:
   200→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   201→                    plan,
   202→                    "sync_import_scores",
   203→                    actor="system",
   204→                    detail={
   205→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   206→                        "injected": bool(import_scores_result.injected),
   207→                        "pruned": list(import_scores_result.pruned),
   208→                    },
   209→                )
   210→            if create_plan_result.changes:
   211→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   212→                    plan,
   213→                    "sync_create_plan",
   214→                    actor="system",
   215→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   216→                )
   217→            if import_result is not None or workflow_injected_ids or covered_ids:
   218→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   219→                    plan,
   220→                    "review_import_sync",
   221→                    actor="system",
   222→                    detail={
   223→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   224→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   225→                        "added_to_queue": (
   226→                            import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   227→                        ),
   228→                        "workflow_injected_ids": workflow_injected_ids,
   229→                        "triage_injected": (
   230→                            import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   231→                        ),
   232→                        "triage_injected_ids": (
   233→                            import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   234→                        ),
   235→                        "triage_deferred": (
   236→                            import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   237→                        ),
   238→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   239→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   240→                        "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   241→                        "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   242→                            import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   243→                        ),
   244→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   245→                        "stale_sync_injected": (
   246→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   247→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   248→                        ),
   249→                        "stale_sync_pruned": (
   250→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   251→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   252→                        ),
   253→                        "auto_cluster_changes": auto_cluster_changes,
   254→                    },
   255→                )
   256→            plan_queue_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   257→
   258→        if import_result is not None:
   259→            _print_review_import_sync(
   260→                state,
   261→                import_result,
   262→                workflow_injected=bool(workflow_injected_ids),
   263→            )
   264→        if injected_parts:
   265→            print(colorize(
   266→                f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   267→                "cyan",
   268→            ))
   269→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   270→        print(
   271→            colorize(
   272→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   273→                "dim",
   274→            )
   275→        )

   277→
   278→__all__ = ["sync_plan_after_import"]

1→"""Shared render helpers for ``desloppify next`` terminal output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import format_plan_delta

    15→
    16→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    17→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    18→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    19→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    20→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",

    22→_CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS = {

    27→
    28→
    29→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    30→class ClusterTypeLabels:
    31→    action_types: dict[str, str]
    32→    cluster_names: dict[str, str]
    33→
    34→
    35→CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS = ClusterTypeLabels(

    41→def scorecard_subjective(
    42→    state: dict,
    43→    dim_scores: dict,
    44→) -> list[dict]:
    45→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    46→    if not dim_scores:
    47→        return []
    48→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    49→        state,
    50→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    51→    )
    52→
    53→
    54→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    55→    issues_scoped: dict,
    56→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    57→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    58→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(issues_scoped)
    59→
    60→
    61→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    62→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    63→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify autofix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    64→
    65→
    66→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    67→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    68→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    69→        return "[review]"
    70→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    71→        return "[auto]"
    72→    return ""
    73→
    74→
    75→def render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    76→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    77→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    78→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    79→        for item in grouped_items:
    80→            confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    81→            tag = effort_tag(item)
    82→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    83→            print(
    84→                f"    [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    85→            )
    86→
    87→
    88→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:
    89→    if cluster_name in CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names:
    90→        return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names[cluster_name]
    91→    return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.action_types.get(action_type, "Grouped task")

    94→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:

    96→    if len(file_counts) <= 5:

    98→        for filename, count in file_counts.most_common():
    99→            print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   103→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
   104→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   105→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3

   118→def cluster_action_commands(cluster_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   119→    """Return semantic cluster action commands independent of terminal labels."""
   120→    return {
   121→        "resolve_all": f'desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   122→        "drill_in": f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",

   138→
   139→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:
   140→    commands = cluster_action_commands(cluster_name)
   141→    print(
   142→        colorize(
   143→            f"\n  Resolve all:   {commands['resolve_all']}",
   144→            "dim",
   145→        )
   146→    )
   147→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      {commands['drill_in']}", "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   149→
   150→
   151→def _step_display_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
   152→    """Extract display text from an action step (string or dict with title)."""
   153→    if isinstance(step, dict):
   154→        return step.get("title", str(step))
   155→    return str(step)
   156→
   157→
   158→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   159→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   160→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   161→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   162→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   163→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   164→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   165→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   166→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   167→    step_badge = f" [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps done]" if action_steps else ""
   168→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   169→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{step_badge}{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   170→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   171→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   172→
   173→    # Action steps
   174→    if action_steps:
   175→        show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
   176→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
   177→            marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   179→        remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   180→        if remaining > 0:
   181→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
   182→
   183→    dep_order = item.get("dependency_order")
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   185→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   186→
   187→    # Cluster members
   188→    members = item.get("members", [])
   189→    if members:
   190→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   191→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   192→
   193→    # Primary action
   194→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
   195→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   196→    if autofix_hint:
   197→        print(colorize(f"\n  Try auto first: {autofix_hint}", "cyan"))
   198→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
   199→    if primary_command:
   200→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   201→
   202→    if is_optional:
   203→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   204→        return
   205→
   206→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   207→
   208→
   209→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   210→    del explain
   211→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   212→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   213→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   214→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   215→    if (
   216→        total == 1
   217→        and len(items) == 1
   218→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   219→    ):
   220→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   221→    else:
   222→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   223→        if total > 5:
   224→            print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
   225→
   226→
   227→def show_empty_queue(
   228→    queue: dict,
   229→    strict: float | None,
   230→    *,
   231→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   232→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   233→) -> bool:
   234→    del target_strict
   235→    if queue.get("items"):
   236→        return False
   237→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   238→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   239→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   240→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   241→        print(colorize(
   242→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   243→            "cyan",
   244→        ))
   245→        print(colorize(
   246→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   247→            "dim",
   248→        ))
   249→        return True
   250→
   251→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   252→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   253→    return True
   254→
   255→
   256→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   257→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   258→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   259→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   260→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   261→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   262→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   264→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   265→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   266→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   267→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS",
   272→    "ClusterTypeLabels",
   273→    "cluster_action_commands",
   274→    "effort_tag",
   275→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   276→    "render_cluster_item",
   277→    "render_compact_item",
   278→    "render_grouped",
   279→    "render_queue_header",
   280→    "scorecard_subjective",
   281→    "show_empty_queue",
   282→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   283→]
   284→

1→"""Tests for stale and unscored subjective dimension sync in the plan."""
     2→

    16→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    17→    plan = empty_plan()
    18→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    19→    return plan

    21→
    22→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    23→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    24→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    25→    assessments: dict = {}
    26→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    27→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    28→            "score": score,
    29→            "strict": score,
    30→            "checks": 1,
    31→            "failing": 0,
    32→            "detectors": {
    33→                "subjective_assessment": {
    34→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    35→                    "placeholder": False,
    36→                }
    37→            },
    38→        }
    39→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    40→            "score": score,
    41→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    42→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_issues_changed",
    43→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    44→        }
    45→    return {
    46→        "issues": {},
    47→        "scan_count": 5,
    48→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    49→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    50→    }
    51→
    52→
    53→def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    54→    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    55→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    56→    assessments: dict = {}
    57→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    58→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    59→            "score": 0,
    60→            "strict": 0,
    61→            "checks": 1,
    62→            "failing": 0,
    63→            "detectors": {
    64→                "subjective_assessment": {
    65→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    66→                    "placeholder": True,
    67→                }
    68→            },
    69→        }
    70→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    71→            "score": 0.0,
    72→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
    73→            "placeholder": True,
    74→        }
    75→    return {
    76→        "issues": {},
    77→        "scan_count": 1,
    78→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    79→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
    84→    unscored: list[str],
    85→    stale: list[str],
    86→) -> dict:
    87→    """Build a state with both unscored and stale dimensions."""
    88→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)
    89→    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)
    90→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale_state["dimension_scores"])
    91→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale_state["subjective_assessments"])
    92→    return state
    93→

    98→
    99→def test_unscored_injected_at_back():
   100→    """Unscored IDs are appended after existing items."""
   101→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_issue::file.py::abc123")
   102→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   103→
   104→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   105→    assert len(result.injected) == 2
   106→    # Existing item keeps its position; unscored dims appended at back
   107→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "some_issue::file.py::abc123"
   108→    assert plan["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")
   109→    assert plan["queue_order"][2].startswith("subjective::")
   110→
   111→
   112→def test_unscored_injection_unconditional():
   113→    """Unscored dims inject even when objective items exist in queue."""

   115→        "some_issue::file.py::abc123",
   116→        "another::file.py::def456",
   117→    )

   119→
   120→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   121→    assert len(result.injected) == 1
   122→    assert plan["queue_order"][-1] == "subjective::design_coherence"
   123→    assert len(plan["queue_order"]) == 3  # 2 existing + 1 unscored

   345→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   346→# Full lifecycle: inject → refresh → prune → re-inject
   347→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   348→
   349→def test_full_lifecycle():
   350→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
   351→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   352→
   353→    # 1. Empty queue, stale dims → inject both
   354→    r1 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   355→    assert len(r1.injected) == 2
   356→    assert plan["queue_order"] == [
   357→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   358→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   359→    ]
   360→
   361→    # 2. User refreshes design_coherence (no longer stale, but still under target)
   362→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   363→
   364→    r2 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   365→    # Not pruned: still under target (score=50)
   366→    assert r2.pruned == []
   367→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
   368→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   369→
   370→    # 3. User raises both scores above target → queue empties
   371→    state["subjective_assessments"]["error_consistency"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   372→    for key in ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"):
   373→        state["dimension_scores"][key]["score"] = 100.0
   374→        state["dimension_scores"][key]["strict"] = 100.0
   375→        state["subjective_assessments"][key]["score"] = 100.0
   376→
   377→    r3 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   378→    assert len(r3.pruned) == 2
   379→    assert plan["queue_order"] == []
   380→    assert r3.injected == []
   381→
   382→    # 4. New mechanical change makes design_coherence stale again
   383→    state["dimension_scores"]["design_coherence"]["score"] = 50.0
   384→    state["dimension_scores"]["design_coherence"]["strict"] = 50.0
   385→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["score"] = 50.0
   386→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   387→
   388→    r4 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   389→    assert r4.injected == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   390→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   391→
   392→
   393→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   394→# Reconcile must not supersede synthetic IDs
   395→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   396→
   397→def test_reconcile_ignores_synthetic_ids():
   398→    """Reconciliation must not treat subjective::* IDs as dead issues."""
   399→    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
   400→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence")
   401→
   402→    result = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
   403→    assert result.superseded == []
   404→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
   405→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in plan.get("superseded", {})
   406→
   407→
   408→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   409→# Injection: only subjective items in queue (relaxed condition)
   410→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   411→
   412→def test_injection_when_only_subjective_in_queue():
   413→    """New stale dims are injected when only subjective IDs remain in queue."""
   414→    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
   415→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   416→
   417→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   418→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in result.injected
   419→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in result.injected  # already there
   420→    assert set(plan["queue_order"]) == {
   421→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   422→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   427→# Auto-clustering of stale subjective dimensions
   428→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   429→
   430→def test_stale_cluster_created():
   431→    """When >=2 stale dims exist, auto_cluster_issues creates a cluster."""
   432→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
   433→
   434→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   435→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   436→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   437→    )
   438→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   439→
   440→    changes = auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)
   441→    assert changes >= 1
   442→    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
   443→
   444→    cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]
   445→    assert cluster["auto"] is True
   446→    assert cluster["cluster_key"] == "subjective::stale"
   447→    assert set(cluster["issue_ids"]) == {
   448→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   449→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   450→    }
   451→    assert "Re-review 2 stale" in cluster["description"]
   452→    assert "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions" in cluster["action"]
   453→    assert "design_coherence" in cluster["action"]
   454→    assert "error_consistency" in cluster["action"]
   455→    assert "--force-review-rerun" not in cluster["action"]
   456→
   457→
   458→def test_stale_cluster_deleted_when_fresh():
   459→    """When all dims are refreshed, the stale cluster is deleted."""
   460→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
   461→
   462→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   463→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   464→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   465→    )
   466→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   467→
   468→    # Create the cluster
   469→    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)
   470→    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
   471→
   472→    # Now remove subjective IDs from queue (simulating refresh + prune)
   473→    plan["queue_order"] = []
   474→
   475→    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)
   476→    assert "auto/stale-review" not in plan["clusters"]
   477→
   478→
   479→def test_stale_cluster_updated():
   480→    """When the stale set changes, the cluster membership updates."""
   481→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
   482→
   483→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   484→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   485→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   486→    )
   487→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   488→
   489→    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)
   490→    assert set(plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["issue_ids"]) == {
   491→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   492→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   493→    }
   494→
   495→    # A third dimension becomes stale — must also appear in state
   496→    plan["queue_order"].append("subjective::convention_drift")
   497→    state2 = _state_with_stale_dimensions(
   498→        "design_coherence", "error_consistency", "convention_drift",
   499→    )
   500→    changes = auto_cluster_issues(plan, state2)
   501→    assert changes >= 1
   502→    assert "subjective::convention_drift" in plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["issue_ids"]
   503→    assert "Re-review 3 stale" in plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["description"]
   504→
   505→
   506→def test_single_stale_dim_no_cluster():
   507→    """Only 1 stale dim → no cluster created (below _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE)."""
   508→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues
   509→
   510→    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")

   512→
   513→    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)
   514→    assert "auto/stale-review" not in plan["clusters"]

I need to add an under_target helper and test. Under_target means: scored (not placeholder), not stale (needs_review_refresh=False), but score < target:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py",
  "old_string": "def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(\n    unscored: list[str],\n    stale: list[str],\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build a state with both unscored and stale dimensions.\"\"\"\n    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)\n    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)\n    state[\"dimension_scores\"].update(stale_state[\"dimension_scores\"])\n    state[\"subjective_assessments\"].update(stale_state[\"subjective_assessments\"])\n    return state",
  "new_string": "def _state_with_under_target_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build a minimal state with under-target (scored, not stale) subjective dimensions.\"\"\"\n    dim_scores: dict = {}\n    assessments: dict = {}\n    for dim_key in dim_keys:\n        dim_scores[dim_key] = {\n            \"score\": score,\n            \"strict\": score,\n            \"checks\": 1,\n            \"failing\": 0,\n            \"detectors\": {\n                \"subjective_assessment\": {\n                    \"dimension_key\": dim_key,\n                    \"placeholder\": False,\n                }\n            },\n        }\n        assessments[dim_key] = {\n            \"score\": score,\n            \"needs_review_refresh\": False,\n        }\n    return {\n        \"issues\": {},\n        \"scan_count\": 5,\n        \"dimension_scores\": dim_scores,\n        \"subjective_assessments\": assessments,\n    }\n\n\ndef _state_with_mixed_dimensions(\n    unscored: list[str],\n    stale: list[str],\n    under_target: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build a state with unscored, stale, and optionally under-target dimensions.\"\"\"\n    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)\n    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)\n    state[\"dimension_scores\"].update(stale_state[\"dimension_scores\"])\n    state[\"subjective_assessments\"].update(stale_state[\"subjective_assessments\"])\n    if under_target:\n        ut_state = _state_with_under_target_dimensions(*under_target)\n        state[\"dimension_scores\"].update(ut_state[\"dimension_scores\"])\n        state[\"subjective_assessments\"].update(ut_state[\"subjective_assessments\"])\n    return state"
}

  "new_string": "def test_single_stale_dim_no_cluster():\n    \"\"\"Only 1 stale dim → no cluster created (below _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE).\"\"\"\n    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_issues\n\n    plan = _plan_with_queue(\"subjective::design_coherence\")\n    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(\"design_coherence\")\n\n    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state)\n    assert \"auto/stale-review\" not in plan[\"clusters\"]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Ownership: sync_unscored does not prune under_target IDs\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_unscored_sync_does_not_prune_under_target_ids():\n    \"\"\"sync_unscored must not remove IDs that are under_target (owned by sync_stale).\"\"\"\n    plan = _plan_with_queue(\n        \"subjective::design_coherence\",   # unscored\n        \"subjective::naming_quality\",     # under_target\n    )\n    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(\n        unscored=[\"design_coherence\"],\n        stale=[],\n        under_target=[\"naming_quality\"],\n    )\n\n    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" not in result.pruned\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n    assert \"subjective::design_coherence\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n\n\ndef test_unscored_mid_cycle_does_not_prune_under_target_ids():\n    \"\"\"Even mid-cycle, sync_unscored must not prune under_target IDs.\"\"\"\n    plan = _plan_with_queue(\n        \"subjective::naming_quality\",     # under_target\n        \"some_issue::file.py::abc123\",    # objective (makes it mid-cycle)\n    )\n    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"strict\": 50.0}  # mark as mid-cycle\n    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(\n        unscored=[],\n        stale=[],\n        under_target=[\"naming_quality\"],\n    )\n    state[\"issues\"][\"some_issue::file.py::abc123\"] = {\n        \"id\": \"some_issue::file.py::abc123\",\n        \"status\": \"open\",\n        \"detector\": \"smells\",\n    }\n\n    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" not in result.pruned\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Regression: full evict → clear backlog → re-injection cycle\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_under_target_reinjected_after_objective_backlog_clears():\n    \"\"\"Under-target IDs evicted mid-cycle must reappear when objective backlog clears.\"\"\"\n    # Start with under_target + objective backlog\n    plan = _plan_with_queue(\"some_issue::file.py::abc123\")\n    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(\n        unscored=[],\n        stale=[],\n        under_target=[\"naming_quality\", \"error_handling\"],\n    )\n    state[\"issues\"][\"some_issue::file.py::abc123\"] = {\n        \"id\": \"some_issue::file.py::abc123\",\n        \"status\": \"open\",\n        \"detector\": \"smells\",\n    }\n\n    # Step 1: sync_stale with objective backlog → under_target not injected\n    r1 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)\n    assert r1.injected == []\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" not in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n\n    # Step 2: sync_unscored should NOT have pruned them if they were somehow present\n    r2 = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n    # Nothing to prune since they weren't in the queue\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" not in r2.pruned\n\n    # Step 3: Objective backlog clears\n    state[\"issues\"][\"some_issue::file.py::abc123\"][\"status\"] = \"done\"\n\n    # Step 4: sync_stale should now inject under_target IDs\n    r3 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" in r3.injected\n    assert \"subjective::error_handling\" in r3.injected\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n    assert \"subjective::error_handling\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n\n    # Step 5: sync_unscored must NOT prune the re-injected under_target IDs\n    r4 = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" not in r4.pruned\n    assert \"subjective::error_handling\" not in r4.pruned\n    assert \"subjective::naming_quality\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]\n    assert \"subjective::error_handling\" in plan[\"queue_order\"]"
}

 sync_plan_after_import(
        state: dict,
        diff: dict,
        assessment_mode: str,
        *,
        state_file: str | Path | None = None,
        config: dict | None = None,
        import_file: str | None = None,
        import_payload: dict | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
        try:
            plan_path = None
            target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
            if state_file is not None:
                plan_path = plan_queue_mod.plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
            if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan(plan_path):
                return
    
            plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
            dirty = False
            workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
            policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(
                state,
                target_strict=target_strict,
                plan=plan,
            )
    
            snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
            current_scores = plan_queue_mod.ScoreSnapshot(
                strict=snapshot.strict,
                overall=snapshot.overall,
                objective=snapshot.objective,
                verified=snapshot.verified,
            )
            trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    
            communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
                plan,
                state,
                policy=policy,
                scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
                current_scores=current_scores,
            )
            if communicate_result.changes:
                dirty = True
                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")
    
            has_review_issue_delta = (
                int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
                or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
                or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
            )
            assessment_keys = (
                imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
                if isinstance(import_payload, dict)
                else set()
            )
            import_result = None
            covered_ids = [
                f"subjective::{dim_key}"
                for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
            ]
            stale_sync_result = None
            auto_cluster_changes = 0
    
            injected_parts: list[str] = []
            if communicate_result.changes:
                injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
    
            import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(
                plan,
                state,
                assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
                import_file=import_file,
                import_payload=import_payload,
            )
            if import_scores_result.changes:
                dirty = True
                if import_scores_result.injected:
                    workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
                    injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
    
            create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(
                plan,
                state,
                policy=policy,
            )
            if create_plan_result.changes:
                dirty = True
                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
                injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
    
            if has_review_issue_delta:
                import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
                    plan,
                    state,
                    policy=policy,
                )
                if import_result is not None:
                    dirty = True
    
            if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:
                # Assessment-bearing imports can change subjective queue semantics
                # even when they add no review findings. Keep queue_order aligned
                # first, then rebuild the derived auto-clusters from that queue.
                cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")

> AGENT

These tests expect auto_cluster_sync to evict — which is exactly the behavior we're removing. The eviction responsibility moves to sync_stale. I need to update these tests to reflect the new ownership model. The tests should now verify that auto_cluster_sync does NOT evict, and that sync_stale handles eviction instead:

---

Now to your question about whether this is a solid approach for ensuring the preflight/postflight sequences work:

**Yes, this is a clean ownership model.** The key insight is that each sync function now has a single domain:

| Function | Owns | Never touches |
|----------|------|--------------|
| `sync_unscored` | Prune/inject **unscored** IDs | stale, under_target |
| `sync_stale` | Prune/inject **stale + under_target** IDs | unscored |
| `auto_cluster_sync` | Create/update clusters, track defer_meta | Queue eviction |

For the **scan → review → score → triage** flow, items will be in proper places because:

1. **Preflight (scan)**: `sync_unscored` injects placeholder dimensions; it now preserves any stale/under_target IDs already in queue (won't accidentally prune them)
2. **Review import**: `sync_stale` handles all stale/under_target injection/eviction using a single `cycle_just_completed` signal — no more conflicting decisions from `auto_cluster_sync`
3. **Score**: When objective backlog clears, `sync_stale` reliably re-injects under_target IDs (the root cause bug is fixed)
4. **Triage**: Queue state is consistent because there's no more "evict then re-inject" race between sync functions

The `SubjectiveVisibility` policy snapshot (computed once per operation) ensures all three functions agree on the objective backlog state within a single sync cycle.

def _sync_stale_and_log(
    plan: dict[str, object],
    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    *,
    policy,
    cycle_just_completed: bool,
) -> bool:
    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
        plan,
        state,
        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
            p,
            s,
            policy=policy,
            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
        ),
    )
    if changed:
        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
    return changed
```

def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
    plan: dict[str, object],
    state: state_mod.StateModel,
    *,
    target_strict: float,
    policy,
    cycle_just_completed: bool,
) -> bool:

        plan,
        state,
        target_strict=target_strict,
        policy=policy,
        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
    )
    if changed:

    return changed

## COMPLETE CALL ORDER SUMMARY

| Order | Function | File | Lines | Condition |
|-------|----------|------|-------|-----------|
| 1 | `scan_queue_preflight()` | cmd.py | 116 | Always (gate check) |
| 2 | `reconcile_plan_post_scan()` | workflow.py | 427 | Always (after state persist) |
| 3 | `_apply_plan_reconciliation()` | plan_reconcile.py | 426 | If plan has user content |
| **4** | **`sync_unscored_dimensions()`** | dimensions.py | 129+ | **Always** |
| 5 | `_subjective_policy_context()` | plan_reconcile.py | 529 | Always (after unscored sync) |
| **6** | **`sync_stale_dimensions()`** | dimensions.py | 195+ | **Always** |
| **7** | **`auto_cluster_issues()`** | auto_cluster.py | 86+ | **Only if NOT mid-cycle** |
| 8 | `sync_triage_needed()` | triage.py | - | Conditional |
| 9 | `sync_communicate_score_needed()` | workflow.py | - | Conditional |
| 10 | `sync_create_plan_needed()` | workflow.py | - | Conditional |

---

## KEY INSIGHTS

1. **`sync_unscored_dimensions`** runs FIRST in the WITHOUT-policy phase (line 263) — before any policy computation
2. **`sync_stale_dimensions`** runs in the WITH-policy phase (lines 276-284) — requires policy context
3. **`auto_cluster_issues`** runs in the WITH-policy phase (lines 479-486) — BUT ONLY if NOT mid-cycle
4. **Mid-cycle protection:** When `plan_start_scores` is set and queue items exist, `auto_cluster_issues` is SKIPPED to preserve user triage state (see line 488-493 message)
5. All three functions log their operations to plan log entries via `append_log_entry()`

1→"""Scan lifecycle orchestration service.
     2→
     3→Keeps scan command flow explicit while delegating stage implementations to
     4→scan_workflow helpers.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Callable
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→from typing import Any
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.workflow import (
    14→    ScanMergeResult,
    15→    ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    16→    ScanRuntime,
    17→    merge_scan_results,
    18→    persist_reminder_history,
    19→    resolve_noise_snapshot,
    20→    run_scan_generation,
    21→)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class ScanOrchestrator:
    26→    """Compose scan lifecycle stages around a resolved ScanRuntime."""
    27→
    28→    runtime: ScanRuntime
    29→    run_scan_generation_fn: Callable[
    30→        [ScanRuntime],
    31→        tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None],
    32→    ] = run_scan_generation
    33→    merge_scan_results_fn: Callable[
    34→        [ScanRuntime, list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None],
    35→        ScanMergeResult,
    36→    ] = merge_scan_results
    37→    resolve_noise_snapshot_fn: Callable[
    38→        [dict[str, Any], dict[str, object]],
    39→        ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    40→    ] = resolve_noise_snapshot
    41→    persist_reminder_history_fn: Callable[
    42→        [ScanRuntime, dict[str, object]],
    43→        None,
    44→    ] = persist_reminder_history
    45→
    46→    def generate(
    47→        self,
    48→    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
    49→        """Run detector generation and lifecycle augmenters."""
    50→        return self.run_scan_generation_fn(self.runtime)
    51→
    52→    def merge(
    53→        self,
    54→        issues: list[dict[str, object]],
    55→        potentials: dict[str, object],
    56→        codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
    57→    ) -> ScanMergeResult:
    58→        """Merge generated issues and persist scan state updates."""
    59→        return self.merge_scan_results_fn(
    60→            self.runtime,
    61→            issues,
    62→            potentials,
    63→            codebase_metrics,
    64→        )
    65→
    66→    def noise_snapshot(self) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
    67→        """Resolve effective noise budgets for display/query payloads."""
    68→        return self.resolve_noise_snapshot_fn(self.runtime.state, self.runtime.config)
    69→
    70→    def persist_reminders(self, narrative: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    71→        """Persist reminder history emitted by post-scan narrative analysis."""
    72→        self.persist_reminder_history_fn(self.runtime, narrative)
    73→
    74→
    75→__all__ = ["ScanOrchestrator"]
    76→

1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
     9→    ScoreDisplayMode,
    10→    plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
    11→    score_display_mode,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import get_plan_start_strict
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    15→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS, CommandError
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import load_plan
    18→
    19→_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    20→
    21→
    22→def scan_queue_preflight(args: object) -> None:
    23→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    24→    # CI profile always passes
    25→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    26→        return
    27→
    28→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    29→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    30→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    31→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    32→            raise CommandError(
    33→                '--force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    34→                "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    35→                'completion"'
    36→            )
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    40→                "Queue-destructive reconciliation steps will be skipped.",
    41→                "yellow",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    47→    try:
    48→        plan = load_plan()
    49→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    50→        _logger.debug("scan preflight plan load skipped", exc_info=True)
    51→        return
    52→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    53→        return  # No active cycle
    54→
    55→    # Count plan-aware remaining items.  Block scan when ANY queue items
    56→    # remain (objective OR subjective).  Mid-cycle scans regenerate issue
    57→    # IDs which wipes triage state and re-clusters the queue, undoing
    58→    # prioritisation work.
    59→    try:
    60→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    61→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
    62→        plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan)
    63→        mode = score_display_mode(breakdown, plan_start_strict)
    64→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    65→        _logger.debug("scan preflight queue breakdown skipped", exc_info=True)
    66→        return
    67→    if mode is ScoreDisplayMode.LIVE:
    68→        return  # Queue fully clear or no active cycle — scan allowed
    69→
    70→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total
    71→    # GATE — block both FROZEN (objective work) and PHASE_TRANSITION
    72→    # (subjective/workflow items remain)
    73→    raise CommandError(
    74→        f"{remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    75→        " remaining in your queue.\n"
    76→        "  Scanning mid-cycle regenerates issue IDs and breaks triage state.\n"
    77→        "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.\n\n"
    78→        "  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):\n"
    79→        '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
    80→        "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    81→        'completion"'
    82→    )
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
    86→

    68→def sync_plan_after_import(
    69→    state: dict,

    82→        if state_file is not None:

    98→            strict=snapshot.strict,
    99→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   100→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   101→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   102→        )
   103→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   104→
   105→        communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
   106→            plan,
   107→            state,
   108→            policy=policy,
   109→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,

   112→        if communicate_result.changes:

   114→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")

   118→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   119→            or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
   120→        )

   122→            imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
   123→            if isinstance(import_payload, dict)

   125→        )

   127→        covered_ids = [

   133→
   134→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   135→        if communicate_result.changes:
   136→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   137→
   138→        import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(

   140→            state,
   141→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   142→            import_file=import_file,
   143→            import_payload=import_payload,
   144→        )
   145→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   146→            dirty = True
   147→            if import_scores_result.injected:
   148→                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
   149→                injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   150→
   151→        create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(
   152→            plan,
   153→            state,
   154→            policy=policy,
   155→        )
   156→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   157→            dirty = True
   158→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
   159→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   160→
   161→        if has_review_issue_delta:
   162→            import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
   163→                plan,
   164→                state,
   165→                policy=policy,

   169→
   170→        if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:

   193→
   194→        if dirty:

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — sync plan queue metadata after a scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     7→
     8→if TYPE_CHECKING:
     9→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan.workflow import ScanRuntime

    13→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    14→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    15→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    16→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
    17→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    18→    ScoreSnapshot,
    19→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    20→    append_log_entry,
    21→    auto_cluster_issues,
    22→    compute_subjective_visibility,

    34→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_deferred_disposition_item
