    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    40→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    41→
    42→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    43→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    44→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    45→    """
    46→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    48→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    49→        return False
    50→    for item in synthetic:
    51→        order.remove(item)
    52→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    53→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    54→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    55→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    56→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    57→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    58→    count = len(synthetic)
    59→    if count:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(
    62→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    63→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    64→                "yellow",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return True
    68→
    69→
    70→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    71→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    72→    return bool(
    73→        plan.get("queue_order")
    74→        or plan.get("overrides")
    75→        or plan.get("clusters")
    76→        or plan.get("skipped")
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    81→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    82→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    83→        return False
    84→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    85→    if recon.resurfaced:
    86→        print(
    87→            colorize(
    88→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    89→                "cyan",
    90→            )
    91→        )
    92→    return bool(recon.changes)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    96→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    97→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    98→    if sync.resurfaced:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   102→                "yellow",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→    if sync.injected:
   106→        print(
   107→            colorize(
   108→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   109→                "cyan",
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→    return bool(sync.changes)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   116→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   117→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   118→    if sync.pruned:
   119→        print(
   120→            colorize(
   121→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   122→                "cyan",
   123→            )
   124→        )
   125→    if sync.injected:
   126→        print(
   127→            colorize(
   128→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   129→                "cyan",
   130→            )
   131→        )
   132→    return bool(sync.changes)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   136→    plan: dict[str, object],
   137→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   138→    *,
   139→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   140→    policy=None,
   141→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   142→) -> bool:
   143→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   144→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   145→        plan, state,
   146→        target_strict=target_strict,
   147→        policy=policy,
   148→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   149→    ))
   150→
   151→
   152→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   153→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   154→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   155→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   156→        return False
   157→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   158→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   159→        return False
   160→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   161→    if scores.strict is None:
   162→        return False
   163→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   164→        "strict": scores.strict,
   165→        "overall": scores.overall,
   166→        "objective": scores.objective,
   167→        "verified": scores.verified,
   168→    }
   169→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   170→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   171→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   172→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   173→    return True
   174→
   175→
   176→def _has_objective_cycle(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    plan: dict[str, object],
   179→) -> bool | None:
   180→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   183→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   184→        )
   185→
   186→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   187→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   188→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   189→        return None
   190→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   191→
   192→
   193→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   194→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   195→) -> bool:
   196→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   197→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   198→        return False
   199→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   200→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   201→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   202→        return False
   203→
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   206→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   207→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   208→            score_display_mode,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   212→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   214→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   215→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   216→        return False
   217→    if not queue_empty:
   218→        return False
   219→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   220→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   221→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   222→    # in the next cycle.
   223→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   224→    return True
   225→
   226→
   227→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   228→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   229→    plan: dict[str, object],
   230→) -> bool:
   231→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   232→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   233→        return False
   234→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   235→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   236→        return False
   237→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   238→        plan,
   239→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def _subjective_policy_context(
   244→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   245→    plan: dict[str, object],
   246→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   247→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   248→
   249→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   250→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   251→        runtime.state,
   252→        target_strict=target_strict,
   253→        plan=plan,
   254→    )
   255→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   256→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   257→
   258→
   259→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   260→    plan: dict[str, object],
   261→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   262→) -> bool:
   263→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   264→    if changed:
   265→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   266→    return changed
   267→
   268→
   269→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   270→    plan: dict[str, object],
   271→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   272→    *,
   273→    policy,
   274→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   275→) -> bool:
   276→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   277→        plan,
   278→        state,
   279→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   280→            p,
   281→            s,
   282→            policy=policy,
   283→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   284→        ),
   285→    )
   286→    if changed:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   288→    return changed
   289→
   290→
   291→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   292→    plan: dict[str, object],
   293→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   294→    *,
   295→    target_strict: float,
   296→    policy,
   297→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   298→) -> bool:
   299→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   300→        plan,
   301→        state,
   302→        target_strict=target_strict,
   303→        policy=policy,
   304→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   305→    )
   306→    if changed:
   307→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   308→    return changed
   309→
   310→
   311→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   312→    plan: dict[str, object],
   313→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   314→    *,
   315→    policy=None,
   316→) -> bool:
   317→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   318→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   319→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   320→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   321→            print(
   322→                colorize(
   323→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   324→                    "dim",
   325→                )
   326→            )
   327→        return False
   328→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   329→        return False
   330→    if triage_sync.injected:
   331→        print(
   332→            colorize(
   333→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   334→                "cyan",
   335→            )
   336→        )
   337→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   338→    return True
   339→
   340→
   341→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   342→    plan: dict[str, object],
   343→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   344→    *,
   345→    policy,
   346→) -> bool:
   347→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   348→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   349→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   350→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   351→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   352→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   353→    )
   354→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   355→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   356→    )
   357→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   358→        return False
   359→    append_log_entry(
   360→        plan,
   361→        "sync_communicate_score",
   362→        actor="system",
   363→        detail={"injected": True},
   364→    )
   365→    return True
   366→
   367→
   368→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   369→    plan: dict[str, object],
   370→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   371→    *,
   372→    policy,
   373→) -> bool:
   374→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   375→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   376→        return False
   377→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   378→        print(
   379→            colorize(
   380→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   381→                "cyan",
   382→            )
   383→        )
   384→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   385→    return True
   386→
   387→
   388→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   389→    plan: dict[str, object],
   390→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   391→) -> bool:
   392→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   393→    if seeded:
   394→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   395→        return True
   396→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   397→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   398→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   399→    if cleared:
   400→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   401→    return cleared
   402→
   403→
   404→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   405→    plan: dict[str, object],
   406→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   407→) -> bool:
   408→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   409→    if changed:
   410→        append_log_entry(
   411→            plan,
   412→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   413→            actor="system",
   414→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   415→        )
   416→    return changed
   417→
   418→
   419→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   420→    *,
   421→    plan: dict[str, object],
   422→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   423→) -> bool:
   424→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   425→    dirty = False
   426→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   427→        dirty = True
   428→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state):
   429→        dirty = True
   430→    return dirty
   431→
   432→
   433→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   434→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   435→
   436→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   437→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   438→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   439→
   440→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   441→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   442→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   443→    """
   444→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   445→        return False
   446→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   447→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   448→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   449→
   450→
   451→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   452→    *,
   453→    plan: dict[str, object],
   454→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   455→    target_strict: float,
   456→    policy,
   457→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   458→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   459→) -> bool:
   460→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   461→
   462→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   463→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   464→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   465→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   466→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   467→    """
   468→    dirty = False
   469→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   470→
   471→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   472→        plan,
   473→        state,
   474→        policy=policy,
   475→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   476→    ):
   477→        dirty = True
   478→    if not mid_cycle:
   479→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   480→            plan,
   481→            state,
   482→            target_strict=target_strict,
   483→            policy=policy,
   484→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   485→        ):
   486→            dirty = True
   487→    else:
   488→        print(
   489→            colorize(
   490→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   491→                "preserve queue state.",
   492→                "dim",
   493→            )
   494→        )
   495→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   496→        dirty = True
   497→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   498→        dirty = True
   499→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   500→        dirty = True
   501→    if not force_rescan:
   502→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   503→            dirty = True
   504→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   505→            dirty = True
   506→    return dirty
   507→
   508→
   509→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   510→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   511→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   512→    try:
   513→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   514→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   515→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   516→        return
   517→
   518→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   519→
   520→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   521→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   522→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   523→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   524→
   525→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   526→
   527→    # Policy must be computed after the without-policy steps, which mutate
   528→    # plan (reconcile/prune) and state (unscored sync) before policy reads them.
   529→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   530→        runtime,
   531→        plan,
   532→    )
   533→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   534→        plan=plan,
   535→        state=runtime.state,
   536→        target_strict=target_strict,
   537→        policy=policy,
   538→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   539→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   540→    ) or dirty
   541→
   542→    if dirty:
   543→        try:
   544→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   545→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   546→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   547→
```

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups issues into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import AUTO_PREFIX
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster_sync import (
     8→    prune_stale_clusters as _prune_stale_clusters,
     9→    sync_issue_clusters as _sync_issue_clusters,
    10→    sync_subjective_clusters as _sync_subjective_clusters,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Repair
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
    21→    """Cross-check cluster membership between cluster.issue_ids and overrides.
    22→
    23→    Repairs three kinds of drift:
    24→    1. Override points to a cluster that does not exist → clear override.
    25→    2. Override points to cluster X, but issue is not in X's issue_ids → add it.
    26→    3. Issue is in cluster.issue_ids but override points elsewhere (or has no
    27→       cluster ref) → update override to match cluster.issue_ids.
    28→
    29→    cluster.issue_ids is treated as authoritative when it disagrees with
    30→    the override, because the sync helpers always write issue_ids first.
    31→    """
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    33→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    34→    repaired = 0
    35→
    36→    # Direction 1: override → cluster that doesn't exist
    37→    for override in overrides.values():
    38→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
    39→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
    40→            override["cluster"] = None
    41→            override["updated_at"] = now
    42→            repaired += 1
    43→
    44→    # Build authoritative map: issue_id → cluster_name from cluster.issue_ids.
    45→    # When an issue appears in multiple clusters, prefer manual (non-auto)
    46→    # clusters, matching the priority in collapse_clusters.
    47→    canonical: dict[str, str] = {}
    48→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    49→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    50→            if issue_id not in canonical or not cluster.get("auto"):
    51→                canonical[issue_id] = name
    52→
    53→    # Direction 2+3: ensure overrides agree with cluster.issue_ids
    54→    for issue_id, cluster_name in canonical.items():
    55→        override = overrides.get(issue_id)
    56→        if override is None:
    57→            overrides[issue_id] = {
    58→                "issue_id": issue_id,
    59→                "cluster": cluster_name,
    60→                "created_at": now,
    61→                "updated_at": now,
    62→            }
    63→            repaired += 1
    64→        elif override.get("cluster") != cluster_name:
    65→            override["cluster"] = cluster_name
    66→            override["updated_at"] = now
    67→            repaired += 1
    68→
    69→    # Direction 2 (reverse): override says cluster X, but issue not in X.issue_ids.
    70→    # If the issue isn't in *any* cluster's issue_ids, clear the override ref.
    71→    for issue_id, override in overrides.items():
    72→        ref = override.get("cluster")
    73→        if not ref or ref not in clusters:
    74→            continue
    75→        if issue_id in canonical:
    76→            continue  # already handled above
    77→        # Override points to a real cluster, but issue is not in any
    78→        # cluster's issue_ids → stale ref, clear it.
    79→        override["cluster"] = None
    80→        override["updated_at"] = now
    81→        repaired += 1
    82→
    83→    return repaired
    84→
    85→
    86→def auto_cluster_issues(
    87→    plan: PlanModel,
    88→    state: StateModel,
    89→    *,
    90→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    91→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    92→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    93→) -> int:
    94→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open issues.
    95→
    96→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
    97→    """
    98→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    99→
   100→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   101→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   102→
   103→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   104→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   105→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   106→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   107→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   108→            if ck:
   109→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   110→
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   113→    changes = 0
   114→
   115→    changes += _sync_issue_clusters(
   116→        plan, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   117→    )
   118→    changes += _sync_subjective_clusters(
   119→        plan, state, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   120→        target_strict=target_strict,
   121→        policy=policy,
   122→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   123→    )
   124→    changes += _prune_stale_clusters(
   125→        plan, issues, clusters, active_auto_keys, now,
   126→    )
   127→    changes += _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   128→
   129→    plan["updated"] = now
   130→    return changes
   131→
   132→
   133→__all__ = [
   134→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   135→    "auto_cluster_issues",
   136→]
   137→

    41→def _subjective_state_sets(
    42→    state: StateModel,
    43→    *,
    44→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    45→    target_strict: float,
    46→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
    47→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""
    48→    if policy is not None:
    49→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
    50→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
    51→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids
    52→    else:
    53→        unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
    54→        stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    55→            state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    56→        )
    57→        under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
    58→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids
    59→
    60→
    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""
    63→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    65→        return set()
    66→    return {
    67→        str(fid)
    68→        for fid in skipped
    69→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    70→    }
    71→
    72→
    73→def _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan: dict) -> None:
    74→    plan.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes
   114→
   115→
   116→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    issues: dict,
   120→    clusters: dict,
   121→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   122→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   123→    now: str,
   124→    *,
   125→    target_strict: float,
   126→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   127→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   128→) -> int:
   129→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   130→    changes = 0
   131→    skipped_subjective_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   132→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   133→    if skipped_subjective_ids and isinstance(order, list):
   134→        overlap = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_subjective_ids]
   135→        for fid in overlap:
   136→            order.remove(fid)
   137→            changes += 1
   138→
   139→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   140→        fid
   141→        for fid in order
   142→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   143→    )
   144→
   145→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   146→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   150→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   151→    )
   152→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   153→        fid
   154→        for fid in all_subjective_ids
   155→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   159→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   160→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   161→        description = (
   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   166→            plan,
   167→            clusters,
   168→            existing_by_key,
   169→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   170→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   171→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   172→            description=description,
   173→            action=action,
   174→            now=now,
   175→        )
   176→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   177→
   178→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   179→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   180→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   182→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   183→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   184→            plan,
   185→            clusters,
   186→            existing_by_key,
   187→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   188→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   189→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   190→            description=description,
   191→            action=action,
   192→            now=now,
   193→        )
   194→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   195→
   196→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(
   197→        fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid
   201→        for fid in stale_state_ids
   202→        if fid not in unscored_state_ids and fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   203→    )
   204→    deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(
   205→        set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids)
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   209→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   210→    ut_prune = [
   211→        fid
   212→        for fid in prev_ut_ids
   213→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   214→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   215→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   216→        and fid in order
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in ut_prune:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        changes += 1
   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   225→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   226→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   227→        description = (
   228→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   229→            f"dimensions under target score"
   230→        )
   231→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   232→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   233→            plan,
   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],
   237→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   238→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   239→            description=description,
   240→            action=action,
   241→            now=now,
   242→            optional=True,
   243→        )
   244→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   245→
   246→        existing_order = set(order)
   247→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   248→            if fid not in existing_order:
   249→                order.append(fid)
   250→
   251→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   252→        if deferred_subjective_ids:
   253→            defer_state = update_defer_state(

   255→                state=state,
   256→                deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids),
   257→                options=DeferUpdateOptions(

   259→                    now=now,
   260→                ),
   261→            )
   262→            defer_state["deferred_stale_ids"] = stale_candidate_ids
   263→            defer_state["deferred_under_target_ids"] = under_target_queue_ids
   264→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   265→                defer_state,

   267→                options=DeferEscalationOptions(

   269→                    now=now,
   270→                ),
   271→            )
   272→            if escalated:

   275→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)
   276→            else:

   278→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   279→        else:

   281→    else:
   282→        _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)

   204→def _apply_assessment_reset(payload: dict, *, source: str, now: str) -> None:
   205→    """Apply a standard reset mutation to a single subjective assessment payload.
   206→

   226→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   227→) -> int:
   228→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   229→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   230→
   231→    reset_keys = {
   232→        key.strip()
   233→        for key in assessments
   234→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   235→    }
   236→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   237→
   238→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   239→    source = "scan_reset_subjective"
   240→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   241→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   242→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   243→            _apply_assessment_reset(payload, source=source, now=now)
   244→            continue
   245→        assessments[key] = {
   246→            "score": 0.0,
   247→            "source": source,
   248→            "assessed_at": now,
   249→            "reset_by": source,
   250→            "placeholder": True,
   251→        }
   252→    return len(reset_keys)
   253→
   254→
   255→
   256→
   257→def prepare_scan_runtime(args: argparse.Namespace) -> ScanRuntime:
   258→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   259→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   260→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   261→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   262→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   263→    path = Path(args.path)
   264→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   265→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   266→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   267→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   268→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   269→            state,
   270→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   271→        )
   272→
   273→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   274→    profile = resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   275→    include_slow_effective = effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   276→
   277→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   278→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   279→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   280→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   281→
   282→    return ScanRuntime(
   283→        args=args,
   284→        state_path=state_file,
   285→        state=state,
   286→        path=path,
   287→        config=config,
   288→        lang=lang,
   289→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   290→        profile=profile,
   291→        effective_include_slow=include_slow_effective,
   292→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   293→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   294→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   295→        force_rescan=bool(getattr(args, "force_rescan", False)),
   296→    )
   297→
   298→
   299→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(
   300→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   301→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   302→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   303→    """Append stale exclude issues when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   304→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   305→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   306→        return issues
   307→
   308→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   309→    stale = audit_excluded_dirs(
   310→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, get_project_root()
   311→    )
   312→    if not stale:
   313→        return issues
   314→
   315→    augmented = list(issues)
   316→    augmented.extend(stale)
   317→    for stale_issue in stale:
   318→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_issue['summary']}", "dim"))
   319→    return augmented
   320→
   321→
   322→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   323→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   324→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   325→    *,
   326→    decay_scans: int,
   327→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   328→    """Append re-triage issues for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   329→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   330→        issues,
   331→        state=runtime.state,
   332→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   333→        project_root=get_project_root(),
   334→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   335→    )
   336→
   337→
   338→def run_scan_generation(
   339→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   340→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   341→    """Run detector pipeline and return issues, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   342→    enable_file_cache()
   343→    enable_parse_cache()
   344→    try:
   345→        issues, potentials = plan_mod.generate_issues(
   346→            runtime.path,
   347→            lang=runtime.lang,
   348→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   349→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   350→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   351→                profile=runtime.profile,
   352→            ),
   353→        )
   354→    finally:
   355→        disable_parse_cache()
   356→        disable_file_cache()
   357→
   358→    codebase_metrics = collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   359→    warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   360→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   361→    )
   362→    issues = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_issues(issues, runtime)
   363→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   364→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   365→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   366→    )
   367→    issues, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_issues(
   368→        issues,
   369→        runtime,
   370→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   371→    )
   372→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   373→    return issues, potentials, codebase_metrics
   374→
   375→
   376→def merge_scan_results(
   377→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   378→    issues: list[dict[str, Any]],
   379→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   380→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   381→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   382→    """Merge issues into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   383→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   384→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   385→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   386→
   387→    if not path_changed:
   388→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   389→    else:
   390→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   391→    prev_dim_scores = (
   392→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   393→    )
   394→
   395→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   396→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   397→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   398→
   399→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
```

    20→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
    21→    """Cross-check cluster membership between cluster.issue_ids and overrides.
    22→
    23→    Repairs three kinds of drift:
    24→    1. Override points to a cluster that does not exist → clear override.
    25→    2. Override points to cluster X, but issue is not in X's issue_ids → add it.
    26→    3. Issue is in cluster.issue_ids but override points elsewhere (or has no
    27→       cluster ref) → update override to match cluster.issue_ids.
    28→
    29→    cluster.issue_ids is treated as authoritative when it disagrees with
    30→    the override, because the sync helpers always write issue_ids first.
    31→    """
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    33→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    34→    repaired = 0
    35→
    36→    # Direction 1: override → cluster that doesn't exist
    37→    for override in overrides.values():
    38→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
    39→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
    40→            override["cluster"] = None
    41→            override["updated_at"] = now
    42→            repaired += 1
    43→
    44→    # Build authoritative map: issue_id → cluster_name from cluster.issue_ids.
    45→    # When an issue appears in multiple clusters, prefer manual (non-auto)
    46→    # clusters, matching the priority in collapse_clusters.
    47→    canonical: dict[str, str] = {}
    48→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    49→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    50→            if issue_id not in canonical or not cluster.get("auto"):

86→def auto_cluster_issues(
    87→    plan: PlanModel,
    88→    state: StateModel,
    89→    *,
    90→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    91→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    92→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    93→) -> int:
    94→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open issues.
    95→
    96→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
    97→    """
    98→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    99→
   100→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   101→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   102→
   103→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   104→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   105→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   106→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   107→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   108→            if ck:
   109→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   110→
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   113→    changes = 0
   114→
   115→    changes += _sync_issue_clusters(
   116→        plan, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   117→    )
   118→    changes += _sync_subjective_clusters(
   119→        plan, state, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   120→        target_strict=target_strict,
   121→        policy=policy,
   122→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   123→    )
   124→    changes += _prune_stale_clusters(
   125→        plan, issues, clusters, active_auto_keys, now,
   126→    )
   127→    changes += _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   128→
   129→    plan["updated"] = now
   130→    return changes
   131→
   132→
   133→__all__ = [
   134→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   135→    "auto_cluster_issues",
   136→]
   137→

## KEY INSIGHTS

1. **`sync_unscored_dimensions`** runs FIRST in the WITHOUT-policy phase (line 263) — before any policy computation
2. **`sync_stale_dimensions`** runs in the WITH-policy phase (lines 276-284) — requires policy context
3. **`auto_cluster_issues`** runs in the WITH-policy phase (lines 479-486) — BUT ONLY if NOT mid-cycle
4. **Mid-cycle protection:** When `plan_start_scores` is set and queue items exist, `auto_cluster_issues` is SKIPPED to preserve user triage state (see line 488-493 message)
5. All three functions log their operations to plan log entries via `append_log_entry()`

   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→

   155→    if not mid_cycle:

   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})

   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.

   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   174→    if mid_cycle:
   175→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   176→    else:
   177→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   178→
   179→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   180→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.

   184→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,

   189→

   191→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   192→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→
   195→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   196→    plan: PlanModel,
   197→    state: StateModel,
   198→    *,
   199→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   200→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   201→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   202→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   203→
   204→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   205→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   206→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   207→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   208→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   209→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   210→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   211→       b. A cycle just completed.
   212→       Never reorders existing items.
   213→    """
   214→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   215→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   216→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   217→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   218→    )
   219→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   220→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   221→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   222→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   223→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   224→
   225→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   226→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   227→
   228→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   229→
   230→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   231→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   232→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   233→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   234→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   235→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   236→    if mid_cycle:
   237→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   238→    else:
   239→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   240→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   241→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   242→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   243→
   244→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   245→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   246→
   247→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   248→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   249→
   250→    return result
   251→
   252→
   253→__all__ = [
   254→    "current_under_target_ids",
   255→    "current_unscored_ids",
   256→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   257→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   258→]

> AGENT

OK, I've traced both paths. Here's my critical analysis:

## Scan Path (sync_unscored → sync_stale → auto_cluster)

**Happy path — backlog clears mid-cycle:**
1. `sync_unscored`: mid_cycle=True, `keep_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids` → preserves them ✓
2. `sync_stale`: mid_cycle=True, no objective backlog → `keep_ids = injectable_ids` → keeps them, `should_inject=True` → re-injects ✓
3. `auto_cluster`: **skipped mid-cycle** in scan path → no cluster, but IDs are in queue for `next` ✓

**Backlog exists mid-cycle:**
1. `sync_unscored`: `keep_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids` → preserves them ✓
2. `sync_stale`: mid_cycle=True, objective backlog → `keep_ids = set()` → **evicts everything** ✓
3. `auto_cluster`: skipped mid-cycle ✓

No conflict between steps 1 and 2 — sync_unscored preserves, sync_stale then evicts. The net result is eviction, which is correct.

**Non-mid-cycle (cycle boundary):**
1. `sync_unscored`: `keep_ids = unscored | stale | under_target` → preserves all ✓
2. `sync_stale`: no backlog → `keep_ids = unscored | injectable`, injects ✓
3. `auto_cluster`: runs, creates clusters ✓

## Review Import Path (sync_stale → auto_cluster, NO sync_unscored)

**Backlog clears after import:**
1. `sync_stale`: no backlog → injects under_target + stale ✓
2. `auto_cluster`: no objective items → creates under-target cluster, redundantly appends IDs (guarded by `if fid not in existing_order`) ✓

**Backlog still exists:**
1. `sync_stale`: `keep_ids = unscored` only → evicts stale/under_target ✓
2. `auto_cluster`: objective items exist → records defer_meta, **no longer evicts** ✓

## Problems I See

**1. Stale docstring in sync_unscored (lines 170-172):**

The comment still says "prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty)" but the code now does `keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids`. This will confuse the next reader.

**2. Escalation contradicts sync_stale — is this intentional?**

In the import path when escalated:
1. `sync_stale` evicts stale/under_target (objective backlog exists)
2. `auto_cluster` → escalated → `_promote_subjective_ids()` re-inserts them at the front

This means escalation *overrides* sync_stale's eviction. That's probably the intent (force visibility after repeated deferrals), but it's the exact kind of cross-authority action we're trying to eliminate. It works because promote runs AFTER eviction, but it's fragile — if the call order ever flips, escalation breaks silently.

**3. auto_cluster_sync still injects under_target IDs into the queue (lines 246-249):**

```python
existing_order = set(order)
for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
    if fid not in existing_order:
        order.append(fid)
```

This is redundant with sync_stale's injection. Harmless today (guarded by `not in existing_order`), but it's a second injection authority — exactly the pattern the plan says to remove.

**4. No sync_unscored in import path — is this safe?**

If a review import scores a previously-unscored dimension (making it under_target), sync_unscored doesn't run to reclassify it. It stays in the queue, and sync_stale's `keep_ids` includes `unscored_ids` so it won't prune it. Auto_cluster rebuilds clusters from current state so the unscored cluster shrinks correctly. **This is fine** — next scan's sync_unscored will clean up.

> AGENT

Right — these tests were relying on auto_cluster to inject under_target IDs into the queue. Now that auto_cluster no longer does that, the tests that call only `auto_cluster_issues` without first calling `sync_stale` won't have IDs in the queue.

The tests need to be updated to reflect the new ownership: sync_stale injects, then auto_cluster creates clusters from what's in the queue.

def test_stale_ids_not_evicted_by_auto_cluster():
    """auto_cluster_sync no longer evicts stale IDs — sync_stale owns that."""
    from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import sync_stale_dimensions

    plan = empty_plan()
    stale_state = _stale_state("design_coherence", "error_consistency", score=50.0)
    plan["queue_order"] = [
        "subjective::design_coherence",
        "subjective::error_consistency",
    ]

    # Step 1: no objective items -> stale IDs remain present
    state_no_obj = {**stale_state, "issues": {}}
    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state_no_obj)
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in order

    # Step 2: objective issues reappear -> auto_cluster should NOT evict
    state_with_obj = {
        **stale_state,
        "issues": {
            "u1": _issue("u1", "unused"),
            "u2": _issue("u2", "unused"),
        },
    }
    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state_with_obj)
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    # IDs remain after auto_cluster — it no longer evicts
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in order

    # But sync_stale DOES evict them when objective backlog exists
    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state_with_obj)
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" not in order
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in result.pruned
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in result.pruned


def test_under_target_lifecycle_with_sync_stale():
    """Full lifecycle: inject under-target when no objective, evict via sync_stale when objective returns."""
    from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import sync_stale_dimensions

    plan = empty_plan()
    ut = _under_target_state("design_coherence", "error_consistency", score=70.0)

    # Phase 1: no objective items — under-target injected via auto_cluster
    state_empty = {**ut, "issues": {}}
    auto_cluster_issues(plan, state_empty)

    order = plan["queue_order"]
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in order
    assert "auto/under-target-review" in plan["clusters"]

    # Phase 2: objective issues appear — sync_stale evicts them
    state_obj = {
        **ut,
        "issues": {
            "u1": _issue("u1", "unused"),
            "u2": _issue("u2", "unused"),
        },
    }
    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state_obj)
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" not in order

    # Phase 3: objective resolved again — sync_stale re-injects
    state_empty2 = {**ut, "issues": {}}
    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state_empty2)
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in order
    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in order</tool_use_error>

> AGENT

**Yes** — there are exactly two entry points for subjective queue management:

1. **Scan postflight** (`plan_reconcile.py`): sync_unscored → sync_stale → auto_cluster
2. **Review import postflight** (`plan_sync.py`): sync_stale → auto_cluster

No other code path injects subjective dimensions into the queue.

**Are subjective dimensions clustered together?**

Yes. `auto_cluster_issues` → `sync_subjective_clusters` creates three possible auto-clusters:

| Cluster | Key | When created |
|---------|-----|-------------|
| `auto/initial-review` | Unscored dims in queue (min 1) | After scan |
| `auto/stale-review` | Stale dims in queue (min 2) | After scan or import |
| `auto/under-target-review` | Under-target dims in queue (min 2), no objective backlog | After scan or import |

**However**, there's a gap in the import path: auto_cluster only runs when `has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys` (line 170 of plan_sync.py). If a review import has neither (pure assessment with no issue delta), the cluster won't be rebuilt. The IDs would still be in the queue individually — just not grouped into a cluster until the next scan.

This is minor — the items surface via `next` regardless of clustering. But it's worth noting: the cluster creation path is slightly narrower than the injection path.

> DEVELOPER

why? shoudl the cluster not be updated always as items are dded to it?

> AGENT

You're right to question it — but on re-examination, the current gating is actually correct. I was wrong to flag it as a gap.

Look at what gates the block (line 170):

```python
if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:
    stale_sync_result = ...sync_stale_dimensions(...)
    auto_cluster_changes = ...auto_cluster_issues(...)
```

Both `sync_stale` and `auto_cluster` are behind the same condition. So:

- If `assessment_keys` exist → an assessment changed subjective state → sync_stale runs (may inject/evict) → auto_cluster rebuilds clusters. Correct.
- If `has_review_issue_delta` → new/resolved objective issues → objective backlog may have changed → sync_stale re-evaluates → auto_cluster rebuilds. Correct.
- If **neither** → no subjective state changed AND no objective backlog change → nothing to inject, nothing to recluster. The existing queue and clusters from the last scan are still valid.

There's no import that changes subjective queue membership without hitting one of those two conditions. An import without issue deltas and without assessments is essentially a no-op for the subjective queue — so skipping both sync_stale and auto_cluster is the right call.

My earlier concern was wrong. The injection and clustering are gated together, so they can't drift apart in the import path.

> DEVELOPER

is this beautifully engineered?

> AGENT

No. It works, but there are two things I'd call out:

**1. The escalation path in auto_cluster_sync contradicts the ownership model.**

sync_stale evicts stale/under_target IDs when objective backlog exists. Then `_promote_subjective_ids` in auto_cluster_sync re-inserts them at the front if the defer counter escalates. That's auto_cluster overriding sync_stale's decision on queue membership — the exact cross-authority pattern this change was supposed to eliminate.

It works only because auto_cluster always runs after sync_stale. If someone reorders those calls, escalation silently breaks.

**2. The docstring at the top of dimensions.py is now wrong.**

Lines 6-9 still describe the old two-function model. It should mention that sync_unscored respects stale/under_target ownership and that sync_stale owns all three categories (stale + under_target + eviction).

The fix for #1 would be to move escalation/promotion into sync_stale itself — it already has the objective_backlog signal and the cycle_just_completed flag. sync_stale should be the single place that decides "are these IDs in the queue or not," including the escalation override. auto_cluster_sync would just track defer_meta and set a flag; sync_stale would read it and act.

Want me to do that?

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:
   101→    """Return updated durable defer metadata for the current deferred ID set."""
   102→    resolved_options = options or DeferUpdateOptions()
   103→    previous = defer_state if isinstance(defer_state, dict) else {}
   104→    current_ids = _normalized_ids(deferred_ids)
   105→    previous_ids = _deferred_ids(
   106→        previous,
   107→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   108→    )
   109→    scan_count = _scan_count(state)
   110→    timestamp = _current_timestamp(state, now=resolved_options.now)
   111→
   112→    updated = dict(previous)
   113→    updated[resolved_options.deferred_ids_field] = current_ids
   114→    updated["last_deferred_scan"] = scan_count
   115→    updated["last_deferred_at"] = timestamp
   116→
   117→    same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids
   118→    if same_ids:
   119→        defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("defer_count"), default=1)
   120→        prior_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("last_deferred_scan"), default=-1)
   121→        if prior_scan != scan_count:
   122→            defer_count += 1
   123→        branch_updates = {
   124→            "defer_count": max(1, defer_count),
   125→            "first_deferred_scan": _coerce_non_negative_int(
   126→            previous.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   127→            default=scan_count,
   128→            ),
   129→            "first_deferred_at": str(previous.get("first_deferred_at") or timestamp),
   130→        }
   131→    else:
   132→        branch_updates = {
   133→            "defer_count": 1 if current_ids else 0,
   134→            "first_deferred_scan": scan_count,
   135→            "first_deferred_at": timestamp,
   136→        }
   137→    updated.update(branch_updates)
   138→    return updated
   139→
   140→
   141→def should_escalate_defer_state(
   142→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
   143→    *,
   144→    state: StateModel,
   145→    options: DeferEscalationOptions | None = None,
   146→) -> bool:
   147→    """Return True when defer metadata crosses configured escalation bounds."""
   148→    if not isinstance(defer_state, dict):
   149→        return False
   150→    resolved_options = options or DeferEscalationOptions()
   151→
   152→    deferred_ids = _deferred_ids(
   153→        defer_state,
   154→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   155→    )
   156→    if not deferred_ids:
   157→        return False
   158→
   159→    min_scans = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_scans))
   160→    min_days = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_days))
   161→
   162→    defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   163→    if defer_count >= min_scans:
   164→        return True
   165→
   166→    first_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(
   167→        defer_state.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   168→        default=-1,
   169→    )
   170→    current_scan = _scan_count(state)
   171→    if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
   172→        return True
   173→
   174→    first_deferred_at = _parse_timestamp(defer_state.get("first_deferred_at"))
   175→    current_at = _parse_timestamp(state.get("last_scan")) or _parse_timestamp(
   176→        resolved_options.now
   177→    )
   178→    if first_deferred_at is None or current_at is None:
   179→        return False
   180→    return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   181→
   182→
   183→__all__ = [
   184→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS",
   185→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS",
   186→    "DeferEscalationOptions",
   187→    "DeferUpdateOptions",
   188→    "should_escalate_defer_state",
   189→    "update_defer_state",
   190→]
   191→
