1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain, and evict them
     9→  again when objective backlog returns.
    10→
    11→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    22→
    23→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# ID helpers
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    32→
    33→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    34→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    35→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    36→    """
    37→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    38→        state,
    39→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→
    43→def current_under_target_ids(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    47→) -> set[str]:
    48→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    49→
    50→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    51→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    52→    """
    53→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    54→        state,
    55→        target_strict=target_strict,
    56→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# Helpers
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:
    77→        order.remove(fid)
    78→        pruned.append(fid)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()
    86→    return {
    87→        str(fid)
    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    90→    }
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:
   104→        order.remove(fid)
   105→        pruned.append(fid)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)
   123→
   124→
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→
   135→    1. **Prune** — always remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order``
   136→       that are no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs —
   137→       that is ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).  Pruning runs even
   138→       mid-cycle to clean up items that were incorrectly injected by older
   139→       code that lacked the mid-cycle guard.
   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """
   144→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   151→    )
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that left ALL subjective categories.
   170→    # sync_unscored only removes IDs it owns (no longer unscored).  IDs that
   171→    # are stale or under_target belong to sync_stale — always keep them.
   172→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→

   defer_state.pop(_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)\n\n    plan[_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state\n    return escalated\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef sync_unscored_dimensions(\n    plan: PlanModel,\n    state: StateModel,\n) -> QueueSyncResult:\n    \"\"\"Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.\n\n    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs that left all subjective\n       categories.  IDs that are stale or under_target belong to\n       ``sync_stale_dimensions`` and are always preserved.\n    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of\n       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —\n       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n    result = QueueSyncResult()\n    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)\n\n    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)\n    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(\n        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,\n    )\n    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)\n\n    if not mid_cycle:\n        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a\n        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).\n        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.\n        skipped_dict = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):\n            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):\n                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)\n                result.resurfaced.append(sid)\n            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids\n\n    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.\n    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)\n\n    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that left ALL subjective categories.\n    # sync_unscored only removes IDs it owns (no longer unscored).  IDs that\n    # are stale or under_target belong to sync_stale — always keep them.\n    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)\n    if mid_cycle:\n        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)\n    else:\n        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)\n\n    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------\n    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.\n    if not mid_cycle:\n        _inject_subjective_ids(\n            order,\n            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,\n            injected=result.injected,\n        )\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef sync_stale_dimensions(\n    plan: PlanModel,\n    state: StateModel,\n    *,\n    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,\n    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,\n) -> QueueSyncResult:\n    \"\"\"Sole authority on queue membership for stale and under-target dimensions.\n\n    Three modes based on objective backlog state:\n\n    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject stale/under-target IDs\n       to the back of the queue.  Clear deferral tracking.\n    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict stale/under-target IDs from queue.\n       Update deferral counter.\n    3. **Backlog, escalated** — override eviction and promote deferred IDs\n       to the front of the queue (after workflow/triage items).\n\n    Always preserves unscored IDs (owned by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n    result = QueueSyncResult()\n    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(\n        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,\n    )\n    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)\n    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)\n    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids\n    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)\n    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n\n    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.\n    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)\n\n    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)\n    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)\n\n    # --- Deferral: track repeated deferrals, check for escalation ---------\n    should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed\n    escalated = False\n    if should_defer and injectable_ids:\n        escalated = _update_deferral(\n            plan, state,\n            injectable_ids=injectable_ids,\n            stale_ids=stale_ids,\n            under_target_ids=under_target_ids,\n            skipped_ids=skipped_ids,\n        )\n    else:\n        _clear_defer_meta(plan)\n\n    # --- Prune: decide what stays in the queue ----------------------------\n    evicting = should_defer and not escalated\n    if mid_cycle:\n        keep_ids = set() if evicting else injectable_ids\n    else:\n        keep_ids = unscored_ids if evicting else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)\n    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)\n\n    # --- Inject or promote ------------------------------------------------\n    if escalated:\n        _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))\n    elif not evicting and injectable_ids:\n        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)\n\n    return result\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"current_under_target_ids\",\n    \"current_unscored_ids\",\n    \"sync_stale_dimensions\",\n    \"sync_unscored_dimensions\",\n]\n"
}

    41→def _subjective_state_sets(

    43→    *,

    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""

    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes

   116→def sync_subjective_clusters(

   135→        for fid in overlap:

   138→
   139→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   140→        fid
   141→        for fid in order
   142→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   143→    )
   144→
   145→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   146→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   147→    )

   151→    )
   152→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(

   154→        for fid in all_subjective_ids
   155→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   159→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   160→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   161→        description = (
   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(

   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"

   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid

   203→    )

   206→    )
   207→
   208→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})

   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:

   228→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   229→            f"dimensions under target score"

   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],

   241→            now=now,

   254→                state=state,
   255→                deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids),

   258→                    now=now,

   262→            defer_state["deferred_under_target_ids"] = under_target_queue_ids
   263→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   264→                defer_state,

   268→                    now=now,
   269→                ),
   270→            )
   271→            if escalated:
   272→                defer_state[_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = list(deferred_subjective_ids)

   274→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)

   278→        else:
   279→            _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   280→    else:
   281→        _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   282→
   283→    return changes

= [\n        fid\n        for fid in prev_ut_ids\n        if fid not in under_target_ids\n        and fid not in stale_state_ids\n        and fid not in unscored_state_ids\n        and fid in order\n len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:\n        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)\n        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]\n > AGENT

5026 passed. Clean.

The ownership table now actually holds:

| Function | Owns | Does NOT do |
|----------|------|-------------|
| `sync_unscored` | Prune/inject unscored IDs | Touch stale, under_target, or defer_meta |
| `sync_stale` | Prune/inject/evict/promote stale + under_target IDs; deferral tracking; escalation | Touch unscored |
| `auto_cluster_sync` | Create/update cluster groupings from queue state | Inject, evict, promote, or touch defer_meta |

> AGENT

There it is. Lines 159-169 — `auto_cluster_sync` is still removing IDs from `order`:

```python
ut_prune = [
    fid
    for fid in prev_ut_ids
    if fid not in under_target_ids
    and fid not in stale_state_ids
    and fid not in unscored_state_ids
    and fid in order
]
for fid in ut_prune:
    order.remove(fid)
```

This is auto_cluster_sync pruning IDs from the queue — the exact thing it's not supposed to do anymore. It's removing IDs that *were* in the under-target cluster but are no longer in any subjective category. That's queue membership management, and it belongs in sync_stale.

And lines 89-94 — the skipped-overlap removal also mutates `order`:

```python
overlap = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_subjective_ids]
for fid in overlap:
    order.remove(fid)
```

That's a third place auto_cluster_sync touches queue membership. sync_stale already does `_prune_skipped_subjective_ids`.

Let me also check: does sync_stale already handle the "left all categories" case that `ut_prune` covers?

   292→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)

   294→
   295→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   296→    plan: PlanModel,
   297→    state: StateModel,
   298→    *,
   299→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   300→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,

   304→    Three modes based on objective backlog state:
   305→
   306→    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject stale/under-target IDs
   307→       to the back of the queue.  Clear deferral tracking.
   308→    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict stale/under-target IDs from queue.
   309→       Update deferral counter.
   310→    3. **Backlog, escalated** — override eviction and promote deferred IDs
   311→       to the front of the queue (after workflow/triage items).
   312→
   313→    Always preserves unscored IDs (owned by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   314→    """
   315→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

> AGENT

Yes — sync_stale's `_prune_subjective_ids` already handles the "left all categories" case. When `evicting=False`, `keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids`. Any subjective ID that's not in unscored, stale, or under_target gets pruned. That covers exactly the same case as `ut_prune`.

And sync_stale already does `_prune_skipped_subjective_ids`. So both of auto_cluster_sync's queue mutations are redundant.

> AGENT

Only reads. auto_cluster_sync is now purely read-only on the plan dict (the cluster mutations happen through the `clusters` dict parameter, which is correct — that's cluster data, not queue membership).

---

Here's where it stands now:

**auto_cluster_sync**: zero writes to `queue_order` or `subjective_defer_meta`. Reads queue state, creates/updates cluster groupings. That's it.

**sync_stale**: sole writer of queue membership for stale/under_target IDs. Sole writer of `subjective_defer_meta`. Handles injection, eviction, deferral tracking, and escalation promotion — all in one place, all in one pass.

**sync_unscored**: sole writer of queue membership for unscored IDs. Never touches stale, under_target, or defer_meta.

The ownership boundaries are enforced by the code structure, not by convention. auto_cluster_sync literally cannot violate them — it doesn't have write access to the queue.

295→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   296→    plan: PlanModel,
   297→    state: StateModel,
   298→    *,
   299→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   300→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   301→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   302→    """Sole authority on queue membership for stale and under-target dimensions.
   303→
   304→    Three modes based on objective backlog state:
   305→
   306→    1. **No backlog** (or cycle just completed) — inject stale/under-target IDs
   307→       to the back of the queue.  Clear deferral tracking.
   308→    2. **Backlog, not escalated** — evict stale/under-target IDs from queue.
   309→       Update deferral counter.
   310→    3. **Backlog, escalated** — override eviction and promote deferred IDs
   311→       to the front of the queue (after workflow/triage items).
   312→
   313→    Always preserves unscored IDs (owned by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   314→    """
   315→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   316→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   317→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   318→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   319→    )
   320→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   321→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   322→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   323→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   324→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   325→
   326→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   327→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   328→
   329→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   330→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   331→
   332→    # --- Deferral: track repeated deferrals, check for escalation ---------
   333→    should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed
   334→    escalated = False
   335→    if should_defer and injectable_ids:
   336→        escalated = _update_deferral(
   337→            plan, state,
   338→            injectable_ids=injectable_ids,
   339→            stale_ids=stale_ids,
   340→            under_target_ids=under_target_ids,
   341→            skipped_ids=skipped_ids,
   342→        )
   343→    else:
   344→        _clear_defer_meta(plan)
   345→
   346→    # --- Prune: decide what stays in the queue ----------------------------
   347→    evicting = should_defer and not escalated
   348→    if mid_cycle:
   349→        keep_ids = set() if evicting else injectable_ids
   350→    else:
   351→        keep_ids = unscored_ids if evicting else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)
   352→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   353→
   354→    # --- Inject or promote ------------------------------------------------
   355→    if escalated:
   356→        _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))
   357→    elif not evicting and injectable_ids:
   358→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   359→
```

}
```

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]

> AGENT

No. It's correct and the ownership is clear, but there's structural ugliness hiding in plain sight.

Both functions sweep the entire `subjective::*` namespace on every call:

```python
# sync_unscored, line 274-277:
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored | stale | under_target, ...)

# sync_stale, line 348-351:
_prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored | injectable, ...)
```

These are nearly identical operations. Both compute `stale_ids`, `under_target_ids`, `unscored_ids`, `skipped_ids` from the same state. Both call `_prune_skipped_subjective_ids`. Both filter every `subjective::*` entry in the queue. The second call always finds nothing to prune because the first already did it.

The "clear ownership" we established is a lie at the implementation level. sync_unscored says "I don't touch stale or under_target" — but it does touch them, it just adds them to `keep_ids`. It still scans them, still reasons about them, still needs to know what they are. That's not ownership separation, that's two functions with the same scope agreeing to be polite.

The beautiful version is one function:

```python
def sync_subjective_dimensions(
    plan, state, *, policy=None, cycle_just_completed=False,
) -> QueueSyncResult:
    # One computation of all ID sets
    # One prune pass
    # Inject unscored (unconditional, non-mid-cycle)
    # Inject/evict/promote stale+under_target (conditional)
    # Deferral tracking
```

One pass, no overlap, no duplicated computation, no temporal coupling between two functions that must agree on the same state.

The reason they're split is that the scan path runs sync_unscored *before* computing the `SubjectiveVisibility` policy, and sync_stale *after*. But sync_unscored doesn't need policy — it only reads state. So the split exists because of a sequencing decision in the caller, not because the logic requires it.

Want me to merge them?

    39→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    40→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    41→
    42→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    43→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    44→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    45→    """
    46→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    48→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    49→        return False
    50→    for item in synthetic:
    51→        order.remove(item)
    52→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    53→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    54→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    55→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    56→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    57→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    58→    count = len(synthetic)
    59→    if count:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(
    62→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    63→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    64→                "yellow",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return True
    68→
    69→
    70→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    71→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    72→    return bool(
    73→        plan.get("queue_order")
    74→        or plan.get("overrides")
    75→        or plan.get("clusters")
    76→        or plan.get("skipped")
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    81→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    82→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    83→        return False
    84→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    85→    if recon.resurfaced:
    86→        print(
    87→            colorize(
    88→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    89→                "cyan",
    90→            )
    91→        )
    92→    return bool(recon.changes)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    96→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    97→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    98→    if sync.resurfaced:
    99→        print(

   102→                "yellow",

   104→        )
   105→    if sync.injected:
   106→        print(

   109→                "cyan",
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→    return bool(sync.changes)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   116→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   117→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   118→    if sync.pruned:
   119→        print(
   120→            colorize(
   121→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",

   123→            )
   124→        )
   125→    if sync.injected:
   126→        print(

   131→        )
   132→    return bool(sync.changes)
   133→
   134→
   135→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   136→    plan: dict[str, object],
   137→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   138→    *,
   139→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   140→    policy=None,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   143→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   144→        plan, state,
   145→        target_strict=target_strict,
   146→        policy=policy,
   147→    ))
   148→
   149→
   150→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   151→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   152→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   153→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   154→        return False
   155→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   156→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   157→        return False
   158→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   159→    if scores.strict is None:
   160→        return False
   161→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   162→        "strict": scores.strict,
   163→        "overall": scores.overall,
   164→        "objective": scores.objective,
   165→        "verified": scores.verified,
   166→    }
   167→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   168→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   169→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   170→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   171→    return True
   172→
   173→
   174→def _has_objective_cycle(
   175→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   176→    plan: dict[str, object],
   177→) -> bool | None:
   178→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   179→    try:
   180→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   181→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   182→        )
   183→
   184→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   185→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   186→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   187→        return None
   188→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   189→
   190→
   191→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   192→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   193→) -> bool:
   194→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   195→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   196→        return False
   197→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   198→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   199→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   200→        return False
   201→
   202→    try:
   203→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   204→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   205→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   206→            score_display_mode,
   207→        )
   208→
   209→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   210→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   211→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   212→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   213→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   214→        return False
   215→    if not queue_empty:
   216→        return False
   217→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   218→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   219→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   220→    # in the next cycle.
   221→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   222→    return True
   223→
   224→
   225→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   226→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   227→    plan: dict[str, object],
   228→) -> bool:
   229→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   230→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   231→        return False
   232→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   233→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   234→        return False
   235→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   236→        plan,
   237→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→
   241→def _subjective_policy_context(
   242→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   243→    plan: dict[str, object],
   244→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   245→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   246→
   247→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   248→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   249→        runtime.state,
   250→        target_strict=target_strict,
   251→        plan=plan,
   252→    )
   253→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   254→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   255→
   256→
   257→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   258→    plan: dict[str, object],
   259→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   260→) -> bool:
   261→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   262→    if changed:
   263→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   264→    return changed
   265→
   266→
   267→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   268→    plan: dict[str, object],
   269→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   270→    *,
   271→    policy,
   272→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   273→) -> bool:
   274→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   275→        plan,
   276→        state,
   277→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   278→            p,
   279→            s,
   280→            policy=policy,
   281→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   282→        ),
   283→    )
   284→    if changed:
   285→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   286→    return changed
   287→
   288→
   289→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   290→    plan: dict[str, object],
   291→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   292→    *,
   293→    target_strict: float,
   294→    policy,
   295→) -> bool:
   296→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   297→        plan,
   298→        state,
   299→        target_strict=target_strict,
   300→        policy=policy,
   301→    )
   302→    if changed:
   303→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   304→    return changed
   305→
   306→
   307→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   308→    plan: dict[str, object],
   309→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   310→    *,
   311→    policy=None,
   312→) -> bool:
   313→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   314→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   315→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   316→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   317→            print(
   318→                colorize(
   319→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   320→                    "dim",
   321→                )
   322→            )
   323→        return False
   324→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   325→        return False
   326→    if triage_sync.injected:
   327→        print(
   328→            colorize(
   329→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   330→                "cyan",
   331→            )
   332→        )
   333→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   334→    return True
   335→
   336→
   337→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   338→    plan: dict[str, object],
   339→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   340→    *,
   341→    policy,
   342→) -> bool:
   343→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   344→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   345→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   346→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   347→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   348→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   349→    )
   350→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   351→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   352→    )
   353→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   354→        return False
   355→    append_log_entry(
   356→        plan,
   357→        "sync_communicate_score",
   358→        actor="system",
   359→        detail={"injected": True},
   360→    )
   361→    return True
   362→
   363→
   364→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   365→    plan: dict[str, object],
   366→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   367→    *,
   368→    policy,
   369→) -> bool:
   370→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   371→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   372→        return False
   373→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   374→        print(
   375→            colorize(
   376→                "  Plan: reviews complete — `workflow::create-plan` queued.",
   377→                "cyan",
   378→            )
   379→        )
   380→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   381→    return True
   382→
   383→
   384→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   385→    plan: dict[str, object],
   386→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   387→) -> bool:
   388→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   389→    if seeded:
   390→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   391→        return True
   392→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   393→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   394→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   395→    if cleared:
   396→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   397→    return cleared
   398→
   399→
   400→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   401→    plan: dict[str, object],
   402→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   403→) -> bool:
   404→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   405→    if changed:
   406→        append_log_entry(
   407→            plan,
   408→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   409→            actor="system",
   410→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   411→        )
   412→    return changed
   413→
   414→
   415→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   416→    *,
   417→    plan: dict[str, object],
   418→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   419→) -> bool:
   420→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   421→    dirty = False
   422→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   423→        dirty = True
   424→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state):
   425→        dirty = True
   426→    return dirty
   427→
   428→
   429→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   430→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   431→
   432→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   433→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   434→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   435→
   436→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   437→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   438→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   439→    """
   440→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   441→        return False
   442→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   443→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   444→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   445→
   446→
   447→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   448→    *,
   449→    plan: dict[str, object],
   450→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   451→    target_strict: float,
   452→    policy,
   453→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   454→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   455→) -> bool:
   456→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   457→
   458→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   459→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   460→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   461→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   462→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   463→    """
   464→    dirty = False
   465→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   466→
   467→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   468→        plan,
   469→        state,
   470→        policy=policy,
   471→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   472→    ):
   473→        dirty = True
   474→    if not mid_cycle:
   475→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   476→            plan,
   477→            state,
   478→            target_strict=target_strict,
   479→            policy=policy,
   480→        ):
   481→            dirty = True
   482→    else:
   483→        print(
   484→            colorize(
   485→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   486→                "preserve queue state.",
   487→                "dim",
   488→            )
   489→        )
   490→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   491→        dirty = True
   492→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   493→        dirty = True
   494→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   495→        dirty = True
   496→    if not force_rescan:
   497→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   498→            dirty = True
   499→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   500→            dirty = True
   501→    return dirty
   502→
   503→
   504→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   505→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   506→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   507→    try:
   508→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   509→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   510→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   511→        return
   512→
   513→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   514→
   515→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   516→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   517→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   518→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   519→
   520→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   521→
   522→    # Policy must be computed after the without-policy steps, which mutate
   523→    # plan (reconcile/prune) and state (unscored sync) before policy reads them.
   524→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   525→        runtime,
   526→        plan,
   527→    )
   528→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   529→        plan=plan,
   530→        state=runtime.state,
   531→        target_strict=target_strict,
   532→        policy=policy,
   533→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   534→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   535→    ) or dirty
   536→
   537→    if dirty:
   538→        try:
   539→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   540→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   541→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   542→
```

1→"""Focused public plan API for queue lifecycle and sync operations."""
     2→

   428→
   429→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   430→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   431→
   432→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   433→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   434→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   435→
   436→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   437→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   438→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   439→    """
   440→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   441→        return False
   442→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   443→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   444→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   445→
   446→
   447→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   448→    *,
   449→    plan: dict[str, object],
   450→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   451→    target_strict: float,
   452→    policy,
   453→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   454→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   455→) -> bool:
   456→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   457→
   458→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   459→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   460→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   461→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   462→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   463→    """
   464→    dirty = False
   465→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   466→
   467→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   468→        plan,
   469→        state,
   470→        policy=policy,
   471→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   472→    ):
   473→        dirty = True
   474→    if not mid_cycle:
   475→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   476→            plan,
   477→            state,
   478→            target_strict=target_strict,
   479→            policy=policy,
   480→        ):
   481→            dirty = True
   482→    else:
   483→        print(
   484→            colorize(
   485→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   486→                "preserve queue state.",
   487→                "dim",
   488→            )
   489→        )
   490→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   491→        dirty = True
   492→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   493→        dirty = True
   494→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   495→        dirty = True
   496→    if not force_rescan:
   497→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   498→            dirty = True
   499→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   500→            dirty = True
   501→    return dirty
   502→
   503→
   504→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   505→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   506→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   507→    try:
   508→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   509→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   510→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   511→        return
   512→
   513→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   514→

1. **Initial request**: User provided a detailed plan to fix subjective queue policy with clear ownership boundaries. The plan identified a bug where under_target items get evicted and never re-injected when objective backlog clears. Root cause: three sync functions (sync_unscored, sync_stale, auto_cluster_sync) manipulate the same queue with overlapping authority.

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user asked me to implement a detailed plan to fix subjective queue policy with clear ownership boundaries. The core bug: under_target items get evicted mid-cycle when objective backlog exists, then never reliably re-injected when the backlog clears. Root cause: three sync functions (sync_unscored, sync_stale, auto_cluster_sync) all manipulate the same queue with overlapping authority. Through iterative critical review, the user pushed for increasingly clean architecture:
   - First: implement the ownership boundary plan
   - Then: move escalation from auto_cluster_sync into sync_stale so sync_stale is the true sole authority
   - Then: remove ALL queue mutations from auto_cluster_sync (including skipped-overlap and ut_prune)
   - Finally: merge sync_unscored and sync_stale into a single function to eliminate duplicated computation and overlapping prune passes

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Subjective dimension queue management (unscored, stale, under_target categories)
   - Queue ownership boundaries: which function can inject/evict/promote IDs
   - Deferral tracking with escalation (defer_count threshold triggers force-promotion)
   - SubjectiveVisibility policy snapshot for consistent backlog assessment
   - Mid-cycle vs cycle-boundary behavior
   - Scan path (preflight/postflight): sync_unscored → policy computation → sync_stale → auto_cluster
   - Import path (postflight): sync_stale → auto_cluster
   - `_prune_subjective_ids` operates on ALL `subjective::*` prefixed IDs with a keep_ids set
   - `_promote_subjective_ids` inserts/moves IDs after workflow/triage items at queue front

3. Files and Code Sections:

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py`** — THE critical file. Was rewritten twice. Currently contains both sync functions plus all deferral/escalation logic moved from auto_cluster_sync.
     - Current state has `sync_unscored_dimensions` and `sync_stale_dimensions` as separate functions
     - Added `_clear_defer_meta`, `_promote_subjective_ids`, `_update_deferral` functions
     - Added defer-meta constants: `_DEFER_META_KEY`, `_DEFER_IDS_FIELD`, `_FORCE_IDS_KEY`
     - sync_stale now has three modes: inject (no backlog), evict (backlog, not escalated), promote (backlog, escalated)
     - Key current code for sync_stale:
     ```python
     def sync_stale_dimensions(
         plan: PlanModel,
         state: StateModel,
         *,
         policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
         cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
     ) -> QueueSyncResult:
         # ... compute IDs ...
         should_defer = objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed
         escalated = False
         if should_defer and injectable_ids:
             escalated = _update_deferral(plan, state, ...)
         else:
             _clear_defer_meta(plan)

         evicting = should_defer and not escalated
         if mid_cycle:
             keep_ids = set() if evicting else injectable_ids
         else:
             keep_ids = unscored_ids if evicting else (unscored_ids | injectable_ids)
         _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)

         if escalated:
             _promote_subjective_ids(order, sorted(injectable_ids))
         elif not evicting and injectable_ids:
             _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
     ```
     - sync_unscored keep_ids now includes under_target_ids:
     ```python
     under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
     if mid_cycle:
         _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
     else:
         _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids | under_target_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
     ```
     - **PENDING**: These two functions should be merged into one `sync_subjective_dimensions` to eliminate duplicated prune passes and ID computation.

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py`** — Stripped to be purely read-only on queue_order.

     - Removed: skipped-overlap queue mutation (lines 89-94 of old code)

   - **`desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`** — Removed `cycle_just_completed` parameter from `auto_cluster_issues` and its call to `_sync_subjective_clusters`.

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/plan_sync.py`** — Added `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")` computation. Passes it to sync_stale_dimensions. Removed it from auto_cluster_issues call.

   - **`desloppify/app/commands/next/render_support.py`** — Changed label `"auto/under-target-review": "Optional re-review"` to `"Below-target re-review"`.
